                        <td style="padding: 28px 36px 18px 36px;">
                            <div style="font-size: 18px; font-weight: 700; color: #0F172A; margin-bottom: 14px;">
                                💡 AI Executive Summary: Key News Insights
                            </div>
                            <table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" width="100%" style="background-color: #F8FAFC; border-left: 4px solid #FF9900; border: 1px solid #E2E8F0; border-left-width: 4px;">
                                <tr>
                                    <td style="padding: 20px 24px; font-size: 13.5px; color: #334155; line-height: 1.7em; text-align: justify;">
                                        <div style='margin-top:2px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>1. 구글-엔비디아 AI 에너지 동맹과 코어위브 루빈 가동 및 800V·연산 효율 혁신</div>구글과 엔비디아가 에메랄드 AI와 손잡고 데이터센터를 전력망 유연성 조절 자원으로 전환하는 'AI 에너지 동맹'을 전격 결성함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMibEFVX3lxTE15OTJ6NjdTOEl1aTB3N08yYkU1Z3JiZjBDNnJjeXBzNHlJeFJ6aWh6ZVBiUGxhWmRPSk1iVXNLWXBFbWNhWDdGX3JuN2txV0RDYnk4NTJ6WHI2MG4wNzF3d1RsN0hnU0k5UVRsWA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[1]</a>. 실시간 연산 부하 제어로 계통 병목을 완화하는 아키텍처를 도입한 가운데 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Nvidia and Google Form AI Energy Alliance, Aiming to Transform Data Centers from Power Hogs into Grid Flexibility Resources&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[2]</a>, 엔비디아는 차세대 베라 루빈과 그록 3 LPX를 투입해 전력망을 직접 제어하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiaEFVX3lxTFBGcllRX2VDMWFFazgydkNoZVpOb3RybEZVUDVmS2YxRDB6Q3lZazduMUwyanM1Um1EY1pVVXVSbEhrcjN4QzFMSnV0TmFIcFJKMDJMUWI5OTdxSy10SzJjZlp1ck55c0hN?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[3]</a> 전력 효율을 극대화함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiaEFVX3lxTE9vLTdUcFhaZ1E0Rjk3bTczRVg3MjBfVWpCUFBHSVAwQzZScnRCd2ZsRElWa1J4bHVhY1o5TnZzMVlJZjZqcVRsMDNxUXFTbVZndFEtQkdaSEV0SVhZazVjdlJqX1dVUTlR?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[4]</a>. 네오클라우드 코어위브는 베라 루빈 NVL72 클러스터를 세계 최초로 가동했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiqAFBVV95cUxNTlN4OENJUEdnYld4S215MmhVWmFiaURicm5vSXJjeUxlYU1lakd6OV9zNnJZZ3lmZFV2NUZqeEJScXkxRlhsMFdfU0YzbHRJUFZHSGFWMnNaUm5aanc3cFRZMEhhQkZOZEtWUVNoLXFfYWxlTGxhMXBvNnFnNjYxTnYzcTZrenRtZlE0TFdpVHowd0Z2WE1qLUcxQU1ZMzB6a0t1OURibWo?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[5]</a>, 아수스는 루빈 기반 액체냉각 서버를 선보임 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=ASUS Unveils NVIDIA Rubin-Powered Liquid-Cooled AI Server for the First Time in Japan at CTC DISCOVER 2026&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[6]</a>. 전력망 부문에서는 슈나이더 일렉트릭이 2.5MW 모듈형 전력 공급 시스템을 공개하고 <a href='https://www.datacenterdynamics.com/en/news/schneider-electric-debuts-25mw-power-modules-for-ai-data-centers/' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[7]</a>, 블룸에너지가 AI DC 전용 800VDC 네이티브 전력 시스템을 출시했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMizAFBVV95cUxORzBuTlktV04yVENjVUNoRjN5dDJUb1UzZ2tVeDYwU2UtMkx5R2NWb1lXVVE3SVhNckpRTk13ZUoxeFNFODJIdXRzTUdjdXg3X0xydkszY1k2YnB6aFlJbzZ4bnRIRDJrTXdIRTlobFJCUjloWEdNMHFiRFlJOEgxMEY1bDhSLXJRU0FWdjNyODA0dTZrT3l4c1hOT2NKbFRmQldvRGJnNnJOV0dDMlZ3VDRmLThvc2JiTlllWmdBbHpoSWxVcWlBUEVzWTbSAdIBQVVfeXFMTUJZcE1ueXgyMGxBcHhjTWYyQVhQVUdBeF94MlNHbGxRUWFrTnR6Q3FUMUZqQzFYdVl2UlZZMXEtV2lFTkpZcmx0YzdHZjhldE16WW5mLXB6cmhnejJBYmRPZE1MRk5jTHpGV1hTa3NSM2xuMmgwa3pvUFlMZEZyZUVsUnE1TjJVMFN0RUczZXJmWDNpVHBMeXlSSUFRN1haVUo4aGhfU3hkaFNMR1VFYlRJQkh3V0NMOXZicmdfQTZmN2MtNTNzOXZWWWJBaFk0cllR?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[8]</a>, 텍트로닉스는 800V 직류 회생형 테스트 시스템을 상용화함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMivAFBVV95cUxNdElLc0F6WXBOV2NhM2JKaXBxR29mU0dYc0dFU0s1QlFRSWFFT1F1YzJXb2pKc1VrYzVoTEMzcXI3YkZNN0xBTTVyemNjWll6ZkRHR3dOSGNqaE9NODdIQklJNEZMeUc3bDdQdXpIUTUxMV9fNUVqdW5rR2d0UDZSNGozcUtteVJLQVBCZjFrNFRueXJ4bWlfc2NIdkM5TVZHcXVQcVBxS29pRTFpejF6RWRQbVZOdVVXS0M5NA?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[9]</a>. 국내에서도 모레(Moreh)가 미국 전시회에서 추론 효율을 극대화하는 '토큰 팩토리 OS'를 공개하고 <a href='https://n.news.naver.com/mnews/article/138/0002241671' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[10]</a>, 한화 트랜스그리드는 전력 관리 AI로 비용 25% 절감을 선언했으며 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZEFVX3lxTE00YU9zYXZ6WXljaVVHUTNfV1QxYmFzbnNtUkxhU2RFS0pBQXNfeDZLcHhsSklfZmJteGs1YUtHU0xpbDJYRjAyVi1ES3liMDNDOVk0THBUQlFRa2d0c2k0MGo2YjM?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[11]</a>, 에이치씨앤지가 CPO 광 결합 원천특허를 등록하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMijAFBVV95cUxQOWR4TFh0b3FKa2pXS2R5bDZpdURUNFR1Q3hwUkpJLVc3Y1FRS2JCdnZmOW5YRTRzSlNTOXhLRHFQWmdyNUY1SExpZjdiMHhUUG1tZnlWZ193MkJGY3FKWVJiOEtBYVdyRHpiVDZNNWZadTBzOWlQV2ZSVGZlb2FIaG56VkNDb1pTVmhnM9IBoAFBVV95cUxPOGJSU0NCX1R2bDdPMTZpWk9La0hzaGhaeDJFSWpvMVpEU2VQZUlNQmVjUkx2N2tpTXNTUlBEMm80Q1FXS2M2bTZDZ1lSX3JXTURacmo0RlpCMzNPMXp2czhtTnBFT2dWczVTMmhxLU5iSHJEODNtUTRnWkV6blFxek9SYXRJZ25WeVhpbm81Y3RLZlNLMkpfRHF3TVRJZzRw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[12]</a> 삼성전자는 초고밀도 스토리지 BM1773을 출시해 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiygFBVV95cUxNTGZPMldsV0pINGhlSTJyVHY5OEZQVTFTSXlnNklzaWdlcGRzaWpJSGJmZzlFaXd5T3BzcXByNkJwTGo5VmRjbGZIaVlGa0pfY3hwQjNoU01saFp6bFBheXp4V0ZfSHMyZzZmaW1VaUtxbnVwRWt0OEJ0ZzNURk9iVkJHVXdaUDVEaVJ1a19pZjZzOG1kYjFlMkZxLVU4aEFkVG13aXBmQTVsZGtnM1YtTDVObFpjTS1vckZ3SDNoR0RBV2wxcXNYSWFR?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[13]</a> 랙당 에너지 효율 제어에 힘을 보탬.<div style='margin-top:18px; margin-bottom:6px; font-size:14.5px; font-weight:700; color:#0F172A; letter-spacing:-0.3px;'>2. 네이버 216MW 진도 태양광 PPA와 SK이노베이션 통합 전력망 및 블루에너지 SMR 신청</div>공공 전력망 포화에 대응해 빅테크들이 자체 기저전원과 재생에너지를 직접 확보하는 온사이트 생태계 구축에 속도를 내는 추세임. 국내에서는 네이버가 싱가포르 거린에너지와 협약을 맺고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTE5EdFd2UG9sVkJzMFpJdXpDQVZjRlZ0RnVXVTVoTVM2OWl5RzVxd3BGYS1NOGdpRDhvSERFSWdEM252MGdwclhnbVFGUndoS2RPR0xEdmRVdjRsWjRSOVU5YWI4RXlHTmliUU9ZdmFZdFI?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[14]</a> 전남 진도 216MW 태양광 단지에 지분 투자를 추진하며 <a href='https://www.etnews.com/20260916000091' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[15]</a> 직접전력구매계약(PPA)을 체결해 데이터센터 전력을 선제 확보함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiUkFVX3lxTE9jY0JoWGtVN1lCZlZIazU4cTc5bTd0VEFhbEtpUk5sYXYta21MU00tcnE4NUYxV0lTM1ZoTHU2Q01yX0h1T3VrYmZLeGx2T1FQVUHSAVNBVV95cUxOeVJxNkZ2VDdlcDRPV1RMM3pDRmZvZXJYNEx5NVFyQ1RyRFpsNC1OVnZkTzFTS2gyUS04ZGRGb0pON1gzeTFQOXRPUTkxWnpqSWJyYw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[16]</a>. SK이노베이션은 'WCE 2026'에서 재생에너지, 대용량 BESS, SMR, LNG를 결합한 AI 데이터센터용 '통합 에너지 솔루션'을 공개하고 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMickFVX3lxTE1KLTlZMUVHT1ZPQ09vWE5kSUFVOExDUnJkeU5WZlFlWVVIN0htX1lzcXhNMHl4YzBzUkpobTI1TW9fbFhiNGZqei1JdHdZOWNlQzdxTGxvUklVcWtmRGNaaWhKNnpOLXFsMVlnNks2RUh2Zw?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[17]</a> 패키지형 전력 공급 사업을 본격화함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMicEFVX3lxTE11T292VElTVlBXLXg1djZTVllnVDVjUjNyOWZXVDBIVF9qMVVsejZ0RXdEWmpVeXlTdV9ISGNmeW1jVGRKQ1NNTmxRMS0wT2hVTHRXdW5ra1JuOUVGY0t1ZWwya0VRUWMzTU5jRUZWd0fSAXRBVV95cUxPX3dwb3dtTDk5SHRqTlRmQXM2OUc2a1lLNk96d2xzRzJ6UC0tZUFYVl95UDN5WWZYcnJ6SS04T3o3X2hnSzlCRUcwQzRJcEVmZTVSZmtqSG0za0NtWUh6bHAyTUpLZ0E1a2pFWVpsaVVPaERZVg?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[18]</a>. 글로벌 시장에서도 온사이트 원전 결합이 가시화되어 SMR 스타트업 블루에너지가 GE 버노바-히타치와 협력해 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=SMR Startup Blue Energy, GE Vernova-Hitachi Seek Federal Construction Permit for Gas-Nuclear Hybrid&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[19]</a> 텍사스 발전소 내 가스-원전 하이브리드 구축용 미국 NRC 건설 허가 신청서를 제출하고 <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=Blue Energy moves Texas gas-plus-nuclear project into NRC construction review, marking a major regulatory milestone&tbm=nws' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7; background-color:#E0F2FE; font-size:11px; font-weight:bold; padding:1px 5px; margin:0 2px; text-decoration:none;'>[20]</a> 크루소 데이터센터에 무탄소 기저전력을 직공급하기로 확정함 <a href='https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMid0FVX3lxTE5MNlNMODlMMU9VTGFqbFY2SENkbHhtXzMtbG03SXVkcGxIQm1TbTFZRm05a0JNNlpvWExLa0R0NGRhZk91X3pOTHRCbzQzYXd2VTBCWmw4SDBxTWp5QS15VmgtNGZjRmRsVnRxOW9LMHgwdllKRnBB?oc=5' target='_blank' style='color:#0284C7;
